FROM
THE LIBRARY
OF
SIR WILLIAM OSLER , BART.

OXFORD

174. MS. in Latin, on paper : written in 1624
by Samuel Bave: 6x4% in., xiv+422
pages: in vellum binding.

Pp. xi-295: ‘Commentarii in Hippocra-
tis Aphorismos septem sectionibus concin-
natos. Samuelis Bavè. Coloniensis. Medi-
cinæ Candidatj Anno 1624...’ With Latin
text of Aphorisms distinguished by larger
writing.

Pp. 305-48: “Ratio Componendi Pul-
verem Marci Cornacchinij quo omnes humani
corporis affectiones ab humoribus copia vel
qualitate peccantibus genitæ tutò citò &
jucundè curantur.’ With chemical and
pharmacological notes &c.

Pp. 359-74: ‘ Rudimenta Chymica cum
selectioribus quibusdam ad quamlibet par-
tem exemplis. A. D. D. Fro: Gillot.’

A number of pp. are blank.

Phillipps MS. 11778. Inserted (Pp. 419-22):
letter from C. W. Shickee, Bath, 29 May, ī914,
giving biographical details of Bave (see also note
to no. 7519). On Cornacchinus see Ferguson,

no. 7040,
